Dispatched fresh, in parallel with andon-defender, always blind to its case and to any prior verdict, and never authored or influenced by the session that proposed or built the fix under review. ([Anselmoo/werkstoff](https://github.com/Anselmoo/werkstoff/blob/HEAD/plugins/andon/agents/andon-challenger.md) · ★1)
- **andon-defender**: Advocates the strongest honest case that a proposed fix satisfies a wire's contract, criterion by criterion, as one blind half of andon-verify's tribunal strategy (strategy a). ([Anselmoo/werkstoff](https://github.com/Anselmoo/werkstoff/blob/HEAD/plugins/andon/agents/andon-defender.md) · ★1)
- **andon-verifier**: Converts andon-defender and andon-challenger claims into reproduced facts by running deterministic checks -- tests, greps, execution -- as the fact-finding third leg of andon-verify's tribunal strategy (strategy a), so both cases rest on evidence rather than assertion. Read and execute only; never edits the artifact under review; never renders a pass/fail verdict itself.
